| REQ-CS-182 | a module that is already listed and comes up again keeps its `:cycle` line, as version 0.13.0 printed it: the second path of a diamond, and the way back to the module asked about. | `tests/test_review_wp2_query.py::DependencyTreeTest::test_a_module_that_comes_up_again_is_marked_as_before` |
| REQ-CS-183 | the query forms `(rdeps "id" depth)`, `(deps "id" depth)`, `(find-callers "fn" depth)` and `(find-calls "fn" depth)` hold every module within the depth, also one a longer path reaches first. | `tests/test_review_wp2_query.py::DependencyTreeTest::test_the_query_forms_hold_every_module_within_the_depth` |
| REQ-CS-184 | `impact <path>` of a path with a source extension that is the file of no module of the graph is never answered for another file whose name is like it (a longer name, a name that holds it, the same stem in another language or letter case): the answer is `(error no-match <path> :suggestions ...)`, or `(error not-in-graph <path> :reason ...)` for a file that exists, with exit status 1; `--json` carries the error for that file; `--sexp` keeps the no-match line of 0.13. | `tests/test_review_wp2_query.py::ExactFileArgumentTest::test_impact_of_a_path_that_names_no_file_of_the_graph_is_an_error` |
| REQ-CS-185 | `impact` answers for the module whose file is exactly the path given: relative to the graph root, absolute, or relative to the current folder when no file of the graph has that path from the root; a module ID is taken as before. | `tests/test_review_wp2_query.py::ExactFileArgumentTest::test_impact_takes_the_path_of_a_file_from_the_root_or_from_the_current_folder` |
| REQ-CS-186 | `usages <file>::Name` answers for the file named exactly, from the root or from the current folder: asked in another folder, the name of a file that is elsewhere answers `(error no-match ...)` with exit status 1, in the three forms, and never the uses of that other file; `--sexp` keeps exit status 0. | `tests/test_review_wp2_query.py::ExactFileArgumentTest::test_usages_of_a_file_that_is_not_in_the_graph_is_an_error` |

| REQ-CS-188 | an argument without a file extension stays a module pattern; its suffix match starts at a path or name boundary, so `core` names app/core and not app/hardcore as well, and `service` names app/user_service. | `tests/test_review_wp2_query.py::ExactFileArgumentTest::test_a_module_pattern_matches_a_suffix_at_a_boundary_first` |
| REQ-CS-189 | an argument that is empty or holds only spaces is an invalid argument (exit status 2) for every command that takes a name, a path or a pattern; none answers for an arbitrary module or prints the whole graph. | `tests/test_review_wp2_query.py::ExactFileArgumentTest::test_an_empty_argument_is_an_error_for_every_command` |
| REQ-CS-190 | `analyze`, `deps`, `rdeps`, `module` and `boundary` take the path of a file of the graph (relative to the root, absolute, or relative to the current folder) where they take its module ID, and answer for that module; so do the query forms. | `tests/test_review_wp2_query.py::FilePathForModuleCommandsTest::test_the_commands_that_take_a_module_take_its_file_path` |
| REQ-CS-191 | `analyze`, `deps`, `rdeps`, `module` and `boundary` exit with status 1 when the file or module asked about is not in the graph: `(error not-in-graph <file> :reason ...)` for a file of the repository, `(error no-match ...)` for any other name, never the answer of a file whose name is like it; with `--json` the document carries the error and the status is the same. | `tests/test_review_wp2_query.py::FilePathForModuleCommandsTest::test_a_file_that_is_not_in_the_graph_is_an_error_with_exit_status_1` |
| REQ-CS-192 | a Python file named index.py is the module of its folder, beside the \_\_init\_\_.py of the package: `impact pkg/index.py` answers for it and names that file, where it named pkg/\_\_init\_\_.py before; `impact pkg/__init__.py` answers for the package file. | `tests/test_review_wp2_query.py::ExactFileArgumentTest::test_a_python_index_file_is_the_module_of_its_folder` |
| REQ-CS-193 | `grep_functions <pattern>` searches the whole body of every function and of every method of a class: a line inside a method is named `<module>::<Class>.<method>` (never by the function defined before the class), a line more than 100 lines below the start of a function is found, and a class of a file without top-level functions is searched. | `tests/test_review_wp2_query.py::GrepFunctionsTest::test_a_match_is_named_by_the_function_or_method_that_holds_it` |

| REQ-CS-195 | `source` and `grep` number the lines of a file as the graph numbers them: a line ends at a line feed (with the carriage return before it) and at nothing else, so a form feed, a vertical tab, a file, group or record separator, NEL, U+2028 or U+2029 inside a line does not shift the numbers of the lines below it; such a character is printed as a space, so an answer keeps one fact a line. | `tests/test_review_wp2_query.py::SourceLineNumbersTest::test_source_and_grep_number_the_lines_as_the_graph_does` |

| REQ-CS-197 | a graph file that holds bytes that are not UTF-8 answers `(error invalid-map ...)` with exit status 1, as any graph that cannot be read; `boundaries --strict` ends with its `boundaries-result` line and exit status 3 (no readable graph), never 1; with `--json` the answer is one JSON object whose first key is `error`. | `tests/test_review_wp2_query.py::ErrorsInPlaceOfTracebacksTest::test_a_graph_file_that_is_not_utf8_is_an_invalid_graph` |

| REQ-CS-199 | a function of the query language called without the set it works on (`(count)`, `(intersect)`, `(diff)`, `(filter)`, `(files)`, `(layer-of)`) answers one `(error invalid-query ...)` line that names the function; with `--json` one JSON object whose first key is `error`. | `tests/test_review_wp2_query.py::ErrorsInPlaceOfTracebacksTest::test_a_query_function_without_its_argument_is_an_invalid_query` |
| REQ-CS-200 | a query nested deeper than the language allows (100 levels) answers one `(error invalid-query ...)` line that says so, whatever its depth; a query of a few levels is evaluated. | `tests/test_review_wp2_query.py::ErrorsInPlaceOfTracebacksTest::test_a_query_nested_too_deep_is_an_invalid_query` |
| REQ-CS-201 | a use file that holds bytes that are not UTF-8 is not read: `usages` answers from the graph, marks the answer `:incomplete unreadable-use-file` and standard error carries the `(warning uses-unreadable ...)` line. | `tests/test_review_wp2_query.py::ErrorsInPlaceOfTracebacksTest::test_a_use_file_that_is_not_utf8_is_said_and_not_a_traceback` |
| REQ-CS-202 | while the use file of a graph has no positions and `usages` reads only the files that hold the name (a repository of over 300 files; WINGBOX_POSITIONS_FILL=0 here), the answer says `:incomplete positions-pending` and lists every file the use lines name: a file that uses the class under another name through a barrel is a row without positions, not left out. `--json` carries "incomplete" and the text for people says it; once the positions are read the answer is complete and carries no mark. | `tests/test_review_wp2_query.py::IncompleteUsagesTest::test_an_answer_made_before_the_positions_are_read_says_so_and_names_every_file` |
| REQ-CS-203 | when the use file of the graph is missing, or belongs to another build of the graph, `usages` says so in the answer (`:incomplete no-use-file`, `:incomplete stale-use-file`; "incomplete" with --json; a line of the text for people) and on standard error, instead of answering 0 uses as if nothing used the symbol; `--sexp` keeps its lines. | `tests/test_review_wp2_query.py::IncompleteUsagesTest::test_a_missing_or_stale_use_file_is_said_in_the_answer` |
| REQ-CS-204 | on one loaded graph (the MCP server keeps it between calls) the answer for a symbol whose users all hold its name carries no `incomplete` mark, also after an answer for another symbol that was incomplete. | `tests/test_review_wp2_query.py::IncompleteUsagesTest::test_the_mark_belongs_to_the_answer_not_to_the_loaded_graph` |
| REQ-CS-205 | the header of a graph file declares its edges (`:edges N`, one per edge fact the builder writes), and a graph that holds fewer edge facts is an invalid graph: a file cut short before or among its edges answers `(error invalid-map "code-graph edge count mismatch: ...")` with exit status 1 for every command, `usages` (which reads the graph for one name) included, and `boundaries --strict` exits 3, where it answered from fewer dependencies before. | `tests/test_review_wp2_query.py::GraphCutShortTest::test_a_graph_with_fewer_edges_than_its_header_declares_is_refused` |
| REQ-CS-206 | a graph whose header states no edge count, or a smaller one than the edge facts it holds (a graph written by hand), is read as before: no edge is missing from it. | `tests/test_review_wp2_query.py::GraphCutShortTest::test_a_graph_that_declares_no_edge_count_or_a_smaller_one_is_read` |
| REQ-CS-207 | in the fact lines of the query commands a module or a file whose name holds a parenthesis, a semicolon or a space is one quoted atom, wherever a module is named (`file`, `dep`, `rdep`, `node`, `analyze`, `fn-def`, an edge), so the line reads as one form and `--json` gives the name as one argument; a plain name is written bare, as before. | `tests/test_review_wp2_query.py::QuotedAtomsTest::test_a_name_that_is_no_plain_symbol_is_one_quoted_atom` |
| REQ-CS-208 | the flat facts of `impact --sexp` and `usages --sexp` write such a name as versions up to 0.13 wrote it (quoted only for a space or a quote), so their lines stay byte for byte what scripts written for those versions read. | `tests/test_review_wp2_query.py::QuotedAtomsTest::test_the_flat_facts_keep_the_atoms_of_version_0_13` |
| REQ-CS-209 | in a pattern a last `$` after a backslash is the character itself, not the end of the line: `grep 'price\$'` finds the lines that hold `price$`; an unescaped last `$` is still the end of the line, also after an escaped backslash. | `tests/test_review_wp2_query.py::SmallFixesTest::test_an_escaped_dollar_at_the_end_of_a_text_is_text` |
| REQ-CS-210 | `entity <name>` with a name no definition is spelled as finds the definitions its pattern matches, the letter case ignored, as `entity '<name>.*'` does: `entity engine` finds the class Engine. | `tests/test_review_wp2_query.py::SmallFixesTest::test_entity_matches_a_name_whatever_its_letter_case` |
| REQ-CS-211 | the command `impact --summary` names for the complete list (`full`) writes a file whose name holds a character a shell reads (a parenthesis, a space) quoted for a shell, in the three forms; a plain file name is written as before. | `tests/test_review_wp2_query.py::QuotedAtomsTest::test_the_command_of_a_summary_quotes_the_file_for_a_shell` |
| REQ-CS-212 | when the graph holds no calls edge for the function (a build without --lsp writes none), `find_callers` and `find_calls` print the lines they printed before on standard output and say on standard error that calls edges need a build with --lsp and that `wingbox usages <fn>` lists the call sites the build resolved; with a calls edge nothing is added. | `tests/test_review_wp2_query.py::CallsHintTest::test_without_calls_edges_the_answer_says_where_calls_are_listed` |
| REQ-CS-213 | A target whose sources are a variable that the same CMake file sets once, at its top level, with literal values has those sources; a variable with one value is also read inside a path. | `tests/test_review_wp6_other_langs.py::CMakeTargetsTest::test_sources_given_through_a_variable_set_once_are_read` |
| REQ-CS-214 | A variable set twice, appended to, set in a block, set after its use, filled by file(GLOB) or set in no place of the file is not substituted, and neither is a generator expression: the target is marked as one whose sources were not all read. | `tests/test_review_wp6_other_langs.py::CMakeTargetsTest::test_a_variable_that_is_not_set_once_literally_stays_unread_and_marks_the_target` |
| REQ-CS-215 | A call written in a bracket comment `#[[ ... ]]` or `#[==[ ... ]==]` of a CMake file creates no target and no test. | `tests/test_review_wp6_other_langs.py::CMakeTargetsTest::test_a_bracket_comment_holds_no_target` |
| REQ-CS-216 | A `#` inside a quoted argument of a CMake call does not start a comment: the call and the calls after it are read. | `tests/test_review_wp6_other_langs.py::CMakeTargetsTest::test_a_hash_in_a_quoted_argument_is_no_comment` |
| REQ-CS-217 | The name of `add_test(NAME "quoted name" ...)` is the text between the quotes. | `tests/test_review_wp6_other_langs.py::CMakeTargetsTest::test_a_quoted_test_name_is_kept_whole` |
| REQ-CS-218 | The `target` fact of a target whose source list was not all read carries `:sources unread`; a target read through a variable set once lists its sources, and a test named with a space is written quoted. | `tests/test_review_wp6_other_langs.py::CMakeAnswerTest::test_the_graph_marks_a_target_whose_sources_were_not_read` |
| REQ-CS-219 | `wingbox impact <file> --only tests` names a target that holds the file through a variable, and after it the targets whose sources were not all read: `(unread-targets <file> <target> ...)`, `unread_targets` in JSON, a line in the text. | `tests/test_review_wp6_other_langs.py::CMakeAnswerTest::test_impact_names_the_target_of_the_variable_and_the_unread_targets` |
| REQ-CS-220 | when the parser raises on one file (RecursionError), the build ends with status 0 and publishes the graph of the other files; it prints one notice with the number of files left out, the file and the error; `.wingbox/graph-unparsed.sexp` holds `(not-parsed-file "<file>" :reason parser-failed)`; the file is not in the build cache; `impact` of it answers `(error not-in-graph <file> :reason parser-failed :language "Python")` with status 1, and `--human` says the parser failed. | `tests/test_review_wp7_builder.py::OneFileCannotStopTheBuildTest::test_a_parser_that_raises_on_one_file_leaves_that_file_out` |
| REQ-CS-221 | a Python file with a `+` chain of 1,000 terms and a Go file with a string concatenation of 1,200 lines do not end the build: status 0, no traceback, the other files are in the graph, and a question about each of the two files is answered either from the graph or with `not-in-graph ... :reason parser-failed`. | `tests/test_review_wp7_builder.py::OneFileCannotStopTheBuildTest::test_long_expressions_of_generated_code_never_end_the_build` |
| REQ-CS-222 | a source file that cannot be opened is left out with `:reason unreadable`; the build ends with status 0 and maps the other files. | `tests/test_review_wp7_builder.py::OneFileCannotStopTheBuildTest::test_a_file_that_cannot_be_read_is_left_out` |
| REQ-CS-223 | when the process that parses one file dies, the files of the other processes and the files not parsed yet are parsed all the same, and only that file comes back as left out (`parser-failed`, the process that parsed it died). | `tests/test_review_wp7_builder.py::OneFileCannotStopTheBuildTest::test_a_worker_process_that_dies_loses_only_its_file` |
| REQ-CS-224 | a nested composer.json with a byte that is not UTF-8 does not end the build: status 0, a warning that names the file, and the imports of the other manifest are resolved. | `tests/test_review_wp7_builder.py::OneFileCannotStopTheBuildTest::test_a_manifest_that_is_not_utf8_is_named_and_the_build_goes_on` |
| REQ-CS-225 | when binding a language raises while it reads a manifest, the manifest is named in one warning and the language is bound again with that file excluded; an error that names no manifest is raised as it is. | `tests/test_review_wp7_builder.py::OneFileCannotStopTheBuildTest::test_binding_goes_on_without_the_manifest_its_reader_fails_on` |
| REQ-CS-226 | a file whose first 8 KB hold a NUL byte is not parsed, whatever its extension: the build leaves it out with `:reason binary`, and `impact` of it answers `(error not-in-graph <file> :reason binary :language "TypeScript")`. A text file is parsed whatever its size, and so is a file whose first NUL byte comes after 8 KB. | `tests/test_review_wp7_builder.py::BinaryContentTest::test_binary_content_under_a_source_extension_is_not_parsed` |
| REQ-CS-227 | the short IDs of two repositories of a workspace never coincide (`data-bridge` with 22 modules next to `db`): the build publishes a graph that loads, and `impact` of a module of `db` lists its importer. | `tests/test_review_wp7_builder.py::ShortIdentifierTest::test_two_repositories_never_share_a_short_identifier` |
| REQ-CS-228 | whatever the repositories are named (a name that ends in a digit, that is the prefix of another, that holds brackets, quotes, spaces or a colon, an empty name), every module has a short ID of its own, made of a prefix that does not end in a digit and a number, and the prefix is an atom; a name that had a usable prefix keeps it (`project` pr, `agent-hub` ah, `proto` next to `project` pro). | `tests/test_review_wp7_builder.py::ShortIdentifierTest::test_a_prefix_never_ends_in_a_digit_and_is_an_atom` |
| REQ-CS-229 | `:edges N` of the graph header is the number of fact lines of the edges section (imports, calls-api, data-flow, refs, calls, http-calls, registry-wires, generated, runs), which is the number of edges of those kinds the reader loads; `:violations` is the number of violation lines. | `tests/test_review_wp7_builder.py::EdgeCountTest::test_the_header_counts_the_edge_lines_of_the_file` |
| REQ-CS-230 | a Kubernetes or Helm manifest of a shape `wingbox infra` does not expect (metadata that is a list, a port that is no number, a name that is no text, a dependency that is no mapping) is skipped with a warning that names the file; the other manifests give their facts and the command ends without an error. | `tests/test_review_wp7_builder.py::InfraTopologyTest::test_a_manifest_of_an_unexpected_shape_is_skipped_with_a_warning` |
| REQ-CS-231 | the type-only check of an import takes the imports the build resolved once per file and resolves none again (a cached rebuild of a large monorepo spent most of its time there). | `tests/test_review_fixes.py::BuildSpeedTest::test_the_type_only_check_resolves_no_import_again` |
| REQ-CS-232 | in any language a deeper row names the file it is reached through and how many other files of that depth lead to it: `through src/billing/invoice.ts (or 1 other file): imports it`; the graph of a TypeScript tree records no item lists. | `tests/test_impact_evidence.py::ImpactEvidenceTypeScriptTest::test_a_typescript_row_at_depth_2_names_the_file_it_is_reached_through` |

| REQ-CSHARP-001 | C# parsing preserves types, methods, bases, attributes, and local type candidates. | `tests/test_csharp_language.py::CSharpLanguageTests::test_parser_extracts_csharp_types_methods_bases_and_usings` |
| REQ-CSHARP-002 | C# local type references resolve through declared namespaces without guessing ambiguous files. | `tests/test_csharp_language.py::CSharpLanguageTests::test_graph_resolves_csharp_namespace_type_references` |
| REQ-CSHARP-003 | C# registration exposes canonical file, output, project, and LSP metadata. | `tests/test_csharp_language.py::CSharpLanguageTests::test_csharp_language_metadata_matches_csharp_ls` |
| REQ-CSHARP-004 | C# discovery excludes generated source under standard .NET build-output directories. | `tests/test_csharp_language.py::CSharpLanguageTests::test_discovery_excludes_dotnet_build_outputs` |
| REQ-CSHARP-005 | An unused C# namespace using does not create a module edge by cardinality. | `tests/test_csharp_language.py::CSharpLanguageTests::test_graph_does_not_guess_from_unused_csharp_namespace` |
| REQ-CSHARP-006 | a C# file is a validation module when it carries a validation attribute of System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations or a base type ValidationAttribute, IValidatableObject or FluentValidation's AbstractValidator, written in full or together with the using directive of the namespace, which the evidence then names; a comment, a string, the using alone or the attribute without the using gives no evidence. | `tests/test_validation_mark.py::OtherLanguagesValidationTest::test_csharp` |
| REQ-CSHARP-007 | A type declared partial in several files of one namespace and project links its user to each of them; other duplicates stay unlinked. | `tests/test_review_wp6_other_langs.py::CSharpTypeBindingTest::test_a_partial_class_links_every_file_that_declares_it` |
| REQ-CSHARP-008 | Partial declarations of one name in two projects are not one type: a file binds the one of its own project, a file of a third project none. | `tests/test_review_wp6_other_langs.py::CSharpTypeBindingTest::test_partial_declarations_of_two_projects_are_two_types` |
| REQ-CSHARP-009 | A C# name binds to a type of an enclosing namespace and of the global namespace. | `tests/test_review_wp6_other_langs.py::CSharpTypeBindingTest::test_a_type_of_an_enclosing_namespace_is_found` |
| REQ-CSHARP-010 | A using directive written inside a namespace block, or after a file-scoped namespace, brings in the types of its namespace. | `tests/test_review_wp6_other_langs.py::CSharpTypeBindingTest::test_a_using_inside_a_namespace_is_read` |
| REQ-CSHARP-011 | A global using of one file brings in its namespace for every file of the same project, and for no file of another project. | `tests/test_review_wp6_other_langs.py::CSharpTypeBindingTest::test_a_global_using_holds_for_the_files_of_its_project` |
| REQ-CSHARP-012 | A name binds to the type of the nearest namespace: a type a using directive brings in gives no edge when the own namespace declares the name, and a name two usings bring in gives none. | `tests/test_review_wp6_other_langs.py::CSharpTypeBindingTest::test_a_type_of_the_own_namespace_hides_the_one_a_using_brings` |

| REQ-DART-005 | part files are their own modules, linked to the library that parts them, and parts name their owner backwards. | `tests/test_dart_language.py::DartLanguageTests::test_part_directives_link_part_files_and_their_owner` |
| REQ-DART-006 | a part of directive with a package: or dart: URI resolves like an import and a missing part is never an error. | `tests/test_dart_language.py::DartLanguageTests::test_parts_named_with_another_library_uri_resolve_through_package_names` |
| REQ-DART-007 | a codegen part that exists on disk is discovered like any other part file, and the missing ones are skipped silently. | `tests/test_dart_language.py::DartLanguageTests::test_generated_part_files_on_disk_are_indexed` |
| REQ-DART-008 | Dart exposes its language server command and label for LSP mode. | `tests/test_dart_language.py::DartLanguageTests::test_dart_language_server_command_is_exposed_for_lsp_mode` |
| REQ-DART-009 | Dart registration exposes canonical file, output, project, and dependency metadata. | `tests/test_dart_language.py::DartLanguageTests::test_dart_language_metadata_matches_the_registry_contract` |
| REQ-DART-010 | a Dart file and its generated codegen part never collide: both keep their own module id. | `tests/test_dart_language.py::DartLanguageTests::test_dart_source_and_generated_files_share_one_module_id` |
| REQ-DART-011 | discovery skips .dart_tool everywhere and build folders by name, and keeps other folders of the same name free of that rule. | `tests/test_dart_language.py::DartLanguageTests::test_discovery_excludes_dart_and_flutter_build_output` |
| REQ-DART-012 | Dart detection without its parser packages names the exact install command and publishes nothing. | `tests/test_dart_language.py::DartLanguageTests::test_dart_only_build_needs_no_parser_packages` |
| REQ-DART-013 | a Dart project becomes a graph of dart nodes that carry their class and methods. | `tests/test_dart_language.py::DartLanguageTests::test_dart_project_produces_graph_nodes_with_dart_entities` |
| REQ-DART-014 | a part named by a package: URI is an edge both ways, while dart: and third-party parts stay external. | `tests/test_dart_language.py::DartLanguageTests::test_package_uri_parts_link_in_both_directions` |
| REQ-DART-015 | tree-sitter-dart is installed with the package except on Windows arm64, where it has no wheel; without it the build skips .dart files with a warning naming wingbox[dart] and still writes the graph of the other languages; a Dart-only project keeps the install error (REQ-DART-012). | `tests/test_dart_optional.py::DartOptionalDependencyTest::test_a_missing_dart_grammar_skips_dart_files_and_the_build_goes_on` |
| REQ-DART-016 | a Dart file is a validation module when a call passes the named argument validator; the evidence names the callee. A comment, a string, a type named FormState or an input formatter gives no evidence. | `tests/test_validation_mark.py::OtherLanguagesValidationTest::test_dart` |
| REQ-DART-017 | A pubspec.yaml with a byte that is not UTF-8 gives a warning; the other packages are read and the graph is built. | `tests/test_review_wp6_other_langs.py::ManifestReadingTest::test_a_pubspec_that_is_not_utf8_is_reported_and_the_build_goes_on` |
| REQ-DART-018 | `part of app.main;`, the form that names the library, makes the part depend on the file that declares `library app.main;`, with the line of the directive; a name two libraries declare is settled by the package of the part, and stays unlinked without one. | `tests/test_review_wp6_other_langs.py::DartPartOfNameTest::test_part_of_a_library_name_links_the_part_to_its_library` |
| REQ-DART-019 | The name of a pubspec.yaml is read in linear time (one line of 100 KB within the bound), and as before: the first `name:` at column 0, without its comment and quotes. | `tests/test_review_wp6_other_langs.py::LinearScansTest::test_the_pubspec_name_is_read_in_linear_time` |

| REQ-DARTACC-009 | a file under test/ is a test module and imports its package's lib code like any other file. | `tests/test_dart_acceptance.py::DartAcceptanceDiscoveryTests::test_a_file_under_test_is_a_test_file` |
| REQ-DARTACC-010 | a nested package's lib file is one module, named by its path from the project root. | `tests/test_dart_acceptance.py::DartAcceptanceDiscoveryTests::test_a_nested_pubspec_does_not_duplicate_its_project` |
| REQ-DARTACC-011 | benchmarks/dart/check_accuracy.py runs on a project path and exits 0 when the graph agrees with the import statements on disk. | `tests/test_dart_acceptance.py::DartAcceptanceAccuracyTests::test_the_accuracy_check_passes_on_a_small_synthetic_project` |
| REQ-DARTACC-012 | check_accuracy.py exits 1 when the graph misses an importer that the files on disk name. | `tests/test_dart_acceptance.py::DartAcceptanceAccuracyTests::test_the_accuracy_check_exits_1_on_a_graph_lacking_an_import` |
| REQ-DARTACC-013 | precision counts a dependent that no import statement backs as extra, while recall counts only the importer pairs of the files. | `tests/test_dart_acceptance.py::DartAcceptanceAccuracyTests::test_the_accuracy_check_counts_only_found_pairs_it_knows` |
| REQ-DARTACC-014 | the sample of a run is reproducible: one seed gives one set of files, a different seed another. | `tests/test_dart_acceptance.py::DartAcceptanceAccuracyTests::test_the_sampled_files_are_the_same_for_the_same_seed` |
| REQ-DARTACC-015 | a build of \~700 Dart files finishes under a generous bound, and a second build of the unchanged tree writes a byte-identical graph. | `tests/test_dart_acceptance.py::DartAcceptancePerformanceTests::test_a_large_project_builds_within_the_bound_and_rebuilds_byte_for_byte` |
| REQ-DARTACC-016 | two full builds of the same tree -- in another directory, with another mtime -- write the same graph, so nothing in it depends on the run. | `tests/test_dart_acceptance.py::DartAcceptancePerformanceTests::test_a_full_rebuild_of_the_unchanged_tree_is_byte_identical_too` |
| REQ-DARTACC-017 | a part directive links two files in both directions and the check counts that link once, as a legitimate edge and not as an extra dependent. | `tests/test_dart_acceptance.py::DartAcceptanceAccuracyTests::test_a_library_and_its_part_are_linked_either_way` |

| REQ-DEPS-008 | a project whose only source files need a parser that is not installed publishes no graph; the error names the pip extra, `pip install "wingbox[<language>]"`, first. | `tests/test_language_parsers.py::SkippedLanguageTest::test_a_project_of_one_language_without_its_parser_stops_with_the_command` |
| REQ-DEPS-009 | a build that skips a language writes one fact per language to .wingbox/graph-skipped.sexp, `(skipped-language csharp :files N :add "pip install \"wingbox[csharp]\"")`; the file is covered by .wingbox/.gitignore and a build that skips nothing removes it. | `tests/test_language_parsers.py::SkippedLanguageTest::test_the_skipped_languages_are_recorded_beside_the_graph_and_cleared_by_a_complete_build` |
| REQ-DEPS-012 | the package depends on tree-sitter, PyYAML and the grammars of TypeScript, Rust, C#, Java, Go, Ruby, PHP, Dart and C/C++, each with an exact pin; the Dart grammar alone carries an environment marker, which leaves it out on Windows arm64, where it has no wheel; the extras csharp, java, go, ruby, php, dart and all stay defined with the same pins for the commands written for 0.12, and `all` holds these parsers and nothing else; the one other extra is `http`, the transport package; every pin equals the one of the language adapter and of the requirements file. | `tests/test_language_parsers.py::PinnedParsersTest::test_the_package_depends_on_every_parser_with_exact_pins` |
| REQ-DEPS-014 | a grammar is imported only from the environment that runs Wingbox: a parser that 0.12 put into \~/.wingbox/parsers (or the folder WINGBOX_PARSERS_DIR names) is not importable after the languages are loaded, and no folder under it is on the module search path. | `tests/test_language_parsers.py::EnvironmentOnlyTest::test_a_parser_in_the_per_user_folder_of_0_12_is_not_read` |

## DOC

| Requirement | Behaviour | Test |
|---|---|---|
| REQ-DOC-001 | the repository is written in English only: no tracked text file may contain Cyrillic letters, in documentation, logs, comments or analyses (benchmark result files and test fixtures excepted, which quote other projects' code). | `tests/test_english_only.py::EnglishOnlyTest::test_no_russian_text_in_the_repository` |
| REQ-DOC-002 | the README is also the package page on PyPI, where a repository-relative link leads nowhere; every Markdown link and image in it is absolute or an anchor of the page. | `tests/test_english_only.py::EnglishOnlyTest::test_readme_links_are_absolute` |
| REQ-DOC-003 | the comparison and find-usages tables of the README are short summaries written by hand; every number in them must be a number of the report its generator wrote from the result files, so the README cannot keep the figures of an older run. | `tests/test_readme_numbers.py::ReadmeNumbersTest::test_summary_tables_use_numbers_of_the_generated_reports` |
| REQ-DOC-004 | docs/assurance/requirements.md is, byte for byte, what `tools/requirements_index.py` writes from the test docstrings of this checkout, so the list of requirements cannot fall behind the tests; `tools/requirements_index.py --check` exits 0 on the repository. | `tests/test_assurance_docs.py::RequirementsIndexTest::test_committed_index_is_what_the_generator_writes` |
| REQ-DOC-005 | the index has one table per area (the id without its number: `CS`, `TS-WS`), areas in name order, and one row per test whose docstring states a requirement (`REQ-<AREA>-<n>: text` between the comment marks): the id, the text on one line with what Markdown would read as markup escaped outside code, and the test as `file::Class::method`; rows follow the number of the id; the page states how many tests and areas it lists and names every test that carries no id. | `tests/test_assurance_docs.py::RequirementsIndexTest::test_one_table_per_area_and_one_row_per_test` |
| REQ-DOC-006 | after a test docstring changes, `tools/requirements_index.py --check` exits 1 and names docs/assurance/requirements.md without writing it; a run without the option rewrites the file, and `--check` then exits 0. | `tests/test_assurance_docs.py::RequirementsIndexTest::test_check_names_a_stale_index_and_a_run_repairs_it` |
| REQ-DOC-007 | every page of docs/assurance cites its sources, and a citation that leads nowhere is a wrong citation: each relative Markdown link of the folder leads to a file or folder of the repository, each test named as `tests/<file>.py::<Class>` exists with that class, and the folder holds README.md, requirements.md, verification-plan.md, known-limits.md and tool-errors.md. | `tests/test_assurance_docs.py::AssurancePagesTest::test_pages_point_at_files_and_tests_that_exist` |
| REQ-DOC-008 | an id belongs to one test: when two tests carry the same id (one behaviour checked twice, or two behaviours under one number), `tools/requirements_index.py` and `--check` exit 1, name on standard error each such id with the tests that carry it, and write nothing, so the index never lists one id for two behaviours; once every test has its own id the index is written; no id of this repository is carried by two tests. | `tests/test_assurance_docs.py::RequirementsIndexTest::test_an_id_that_two_tests_carry_is_refused` |
| REQ-DOC-009 | beside its table, the comparison section of the README names the Wingbox versions that benchmarks/graphify/results/versions.txt records for the tests the table summarises (`wingbox_test_b` for the FastAPI row, `wingbox_test_c` and `wingbox_test_d` for the others) and no other, and its sentence on the size of the answers gives the two numbers of the same sentence of the generated report, which are the means of `tokens_chars_div_4` over the questions CodeGraph answers in the result files. | `tests/test_readme_numbers.py::ReadmeNumbersTest::test_the_sentences_around_the_comparison_table_follow_the_result_files` |
| REQ-DOC-010 | an installed skill holds SKILL.md and references/ and no script, so every example of SKILL.md is written as `wingbox <command>`: no line of a shell block runs `python3` on a script or names SKILL_ROOT or TARGET_ROOT, the script paths stand only in the section "Scripts", which says that they are in a clone of the repository, and every `wingbox <name>` of a shell block is a command the program knows (the commands of its help and the query commands it lists for an unknown one). | `tests/test_review_wp9_mcp_ci_docs.py::SkillTextTest::test_the_examples_of_the_skill_run_the_command_not_scripts_it_does_not_hold` |
| REQ-DOC-011 | .wingbox/index.sexp maps every module path to a short ID once, in its `(paths ...)` form, and its facts name modules by those IDs, so a text search of the file for a path finds no fact; SKILL.md therefore gives no `rg` line over the graph file for a module, and its lookups work: `module <id> --lines` prints the import with both paths and its line, `rdeps` names the importer, `search <fact> --lines` prints the stored fact with short IDs, and `module <short id>` names the module of one; references/sexp-notation.md states the mapping. | `tests/test_review_wp9_mcp_ci_docs.py::SkillTextTest::test_the_graph_file_names_modules_by_short_id_and_the_documents_say_so` |
| REQ-DOC-012 | AGENTS.md, section 1, names as supported the operating systems that the classifiers of pyproject.toml declare and the matrix of tests.yml runs: macOS, Linux and Windows. | `tests/test_review_wp9_mcp_ci_docs.py::GuideTextTest::test_the_contributor_guide_names_the_systems_the_package_supports` |
| REQ-DOC-013 | the row "Use positions outside ..." of docs/assurance/known-limits.md and the sentence of the README on use positions name the languages whose parser has a use-position walk (`<family>_use_positions` in scripts/lang: Python, TypeScript with JavaScript, Rust, and C and C++ through the cpp parser), and no other. | `tests/test_review_wp9_mcp_ci_docs.py::GuideTextTest::test_the_limits_page_names_every_language_with_use_positions` |
| REQ-DOC-014 | where the README says that building and querying open no network connection and that the code of a repository is never run, it names the exception: `wingbox build --lsp`, off by default, starts a language server, which can run the build of the project and reach a package registry; the bullet names the command of every language server a language adapter defines, and SKILL.md says the same where it shows `--lsp`. | `tests/test_review_wp9_mcp_ci_docs.py::GuideTextTest::test_the_readme_names_the_one_option_under_which_code_may_run` |
| REQ-DOC-015 | every version ROADMAP.md names under "Recently shipped" is a release of CHANGELOG.md, newest first, and the roadmap says that the pull request check (the GitHub Action `aileron-tech/wingbox-action`) is published, in that list and in the bullet on GitHub and GitLab, which no longer announces it as something to come. | `tests/test_review_wp9_mcp_ci_docs.py::GuideTextTest::test_the_roadmap_lists_released_versions_and_the_published_check` |

## FRESH

| Requirement | Behaviour | Test |
|---|---|---|
| REQ-FRESH-001 | after an edit that adds an import, a partial update must publish the same graph, use file, cache and fragment store as a full build, and redo fewer modules than the project has. | `tests/test_graph_update.py::PartialUpdateTest::test_an_edit_that_adds_an_import_is_patched_like_a_full_build` |
| REQ-FRESH-002 | a created module and an edit that imports it must be patched like a full build. | `tests/test_graph_update.py::PartialUpdateTest::test_a_created_module_and_its_importer` |
| REQ-FRESH-003 | creating the module an unchanged file already imports must redo that importer: same graph as a full build. | `tests/test_graph_update.py::PartialUpdateTest::test_a_created_module_that_an_existing_import_was_waiting_for` |
| REQ-FRESH-004 | deleting a module others import must remove it and its edges like a full build. | `tests/test_graph_update.py::PartialUpdateTest::test_a_deleted_module` |
| REQ-FRESH-005 | a rename (old path deleted, new path created), then the importers fixed, must each give the graph of a full build. | `tests/test_graph_update.py::PartialUpdateTest::test_a_renamed_module_with_and_without_its_importers_fixed` |
| REQ-FRESH-006 | renaming an export that importers reach through a re-export barrel must redo them: same graph as a full build. | `tests/test_graph_update.py::PartialUpdateTest::test_an_export_change_reaches_the_files_that_use_it_through_a_barrel` |
| REQ-FRESH-007 | a changed package.json, tsconfig.json or pyproject.toml, or a changed source of a language whose imports depend on other files' contents, must not be patched (NotApplicable). | `tests/test_graph_update.py::PartialUpdateTest::test_a_resolution_config_or_another_language_takes_an_incremental_build` |
| REQ-FRESH-008 | a created source that the Git ignore rules exclude must stay out of the patched graph, as discovery leaves it out. | `tests/test_graph_update.py::PartialUpdateTest::test_a_created_file_under_git_ignore_rules_stays_out` |
| REQ-FRESH-009 | when the published graph was built after the fragment store (another key), the update must not apply. | `tests/test_graph_update.py::PartialUpdateTest::test_a_store_that_does_not_match_the_graph_is_not_used` |
| REQ-FRESH-010 | resolving the modules in forked worker processes must give the same nodes and edges as one process. | `tests/test_graph_update.py::PartialUpdateTest::test_parallel_resolution_gives_the_same_graph` |
| REQ-FRESH-011 | install must add one PostToolUse group on Edit\|Write\|MultiEdit running `<same program> hook dirty`, one PostToolUse group on Bash running `hook git` and one SessionStart group running `hook session-start`; installing over a settings file from an older install (search hook only, timeout 5) must leave exactly one Wingbox group per hook, and uninstall must remove all of them and keep the other hooks. | `tests/test_install.py::GlobalInstallTest::test_install_adds_the_edit_hook_and_reinstall_upgrades_an_old_install_without_duplicates` |
| REQ-FRESH-012 | the Claude Code manifest must carry a PostToolUse hook on Edit\|Write\|MultiEdit running `wingbox hook dirty`, a PostToolUse hook on Bash running `wingbox hook git` and a SessionStart hook (no matcher) running `wingbox hook session-start`, each as `uvx --offline --quiet --from <pin> wingbox hook ...` in exec form with `"onFailure": "continue"` and a timeout of 5 seconds, and the search hook a timeout of 10 seconds (it may wait for a refresh of the graph). | `tests/test_plugins.py::ClaudePluginTest::test_edit_hook` |
| REQ-FRESH-013 | after an Edit that adds an import (PostToolUse `hook dirty`), the next PreToolUse context must already count the new dependent; the marks are cleared. | `tests/test_freshness.py::ClaudeCodeEditsTest::test_an_edit_that_adds_an_import_is_in_the_graph_before_its_next_use` |
| REQ-FRESH-014 | a module created by Write must be in the graph at the next use; a deleted module (Bash rm) must be gone from it. | `tests/test_freshness.py::ClaudeCodeEditsTest::test_a_written_module_appears_and_a_deleted_one_disappears` |
| REQ-FRESH-015 | after a rename (git mv) and the importer's edit, the old path must be gone and the new one counted. | `tests/test_freshness.py::ClaudeCodeEditsTest::test_a_renamed_module_is_counted_under_its_new_path_only` |
| REQ-FRESH-016 | three edits before a search must be refreshed once; searches on an unchanged project must start no refresh at all. | `tests/test_freshness.py::ClaudeCodeEditsTest::test_several_edits_take_one_refresh_and_an_unchanged_project_none` |
| REQ-FRESH-017 | eight PostToolUse hooks running at once must leave all eight paths marked. | `tests/test_freshness.py::ClaudeCodeEditsTest::test_parallel_edit_hooks_lose_no_change` |
| REQ-FRESH-018 | when the refresh fails, the marks must stay, the hook must give no context from the old graph but `(wingbox-graph-stale :reason refresh-failed ...)`, and no second refresh may run until something changes; a later change refreshes again. | `tests/test_freshness.py::ClaudeCodeEditsTest::test_a_failed_refresh_keeps_the_marks_says_so_and_is_not_repeated` |
| REQ-FRESH-019 | with WINGBOX_HOOK_WAIT=0 the hook must answer `(wingbox-graph-stale :reason refresh-timeout ...)` without context and keep the marks; the refresh it started must then publish the change and clear them. | `tests/test_freshness.py::ClaudeCodeEditsTest::test_a_refresh_longer_than_the_wait_skips_the_context_and_finishes_in_the_background` |
| REQ-FRESH-020 | an Edit of a file that is not a source or resolution config (README.md), outside the project, or a failed Write that left the file as it was, must not mark the graph. | `tests/test_freshness.py::ClaudeCodeEditsTest::test_edits_outside_the_analysed_sources_mark_nothing` |
| REQ-FRESH-021 | an MCP tool called after an edit must answer from the refreshed graph (its cache reloads the new file). | `tests/test_freshness.py::ClaudeCodeEditsTest::test_the_mcp_tools_answer_from_the_newest_graph` |
| REQ-FRESH-022 | `wingbox impact` after an outside change must answer from the refreshed graph; with WINGBOX_AUTO_REFRESH=0 it must warn on stderr that the graph may miss changes. | `tests/test_freshness.py::ClaudeCodeEditsTest::test_a_query_command_refreshes_and_says_when_it_could_not` |

| REQ-FRESH-057 | after init adds a project .gitignore, the next search hook refreshes the graph and supplies context even when the project's name is outside the inherited output encoding. | `tests/test_freshness.py::ConcurrentRefreshTest::test_init_can_refresh_in_a_non_ascii_project_with_legacy_output_encoding` |
| REQ-FRESH-058 | after tsconfig.json maps an alias to another folder, the incremental build publishes the graph of a build from nothing, and so does the partial update of a later edit of an unrelated file; `usages` of the newly aliased definition gives the same answer in both. | `tests/test_review_wp7_builder.py::ResolutionConfigChangeTest::test_a_remapped_typescript_alias_stays_remapped_after_an_unrelated_edit` |
| REQ-FRESH-059 | `usages` asked before a tsconfig.json change fills the use file and the walk cache; after the change and a build, `usages` of the old and of the new target answer as after a build from nothing. | `tests/test_review_wp7_builder.py::ResolutionConfigChangeTest::test_usages_asked_before_the_alias_moved_are_not_answered_again` |
| REQ-FRESH-060 | after composer.json moves a PSR-4 namespace to another folder, the incremental build and the partial update of a later edit of an unrelated file publish the graph of a build from nothing, with the same `usages` answer. | `tests/test_review_wp7_builder.py::ResolutionConfigChangeTest::test_a_moved_composer_namespace_root_stays_moved_after_an_unrelated_edit` |
| REQ-FRESH-061 | after compile_commands.json names another include folder, the incremental build and the partial update of a later edit of an unrelated file publish the graph of a build from nothing, with the same `usages` answer. | `tests/test_review_wp7_builder.py::ResolutionConfigChangeTest::test_another_include_folder_of_the_compilation_database_stays_after_an_unrelated_edit` |
| REQ-FRESH-062 | a change of a file the build left out is never patched into the graph (the partial update does not apply, the build decides again), a changed file that cannot be parsed now is not patched in either, and a build that parses every file removes the record. | `tests/test_review_wp7_builder.py::OneFileCannotStopTheBuildTest::test_the_record_follows_the_next_build_and_no_patch_touches_such_a_file` |
| REQ-FRESH-063 | a symbolic link with a source extension, or a source file below a linked folder, that appears after the build is not patched into the graph: discovery follows no link, so the partial update leaves it out and the graph holds the modules a build from nothing holds; a source file replaced by a link leaves the graph. | `tests/test_review_wp7_builder.py::LinkedSourceTest::test_a_link_created_after_the_build_is_not_patched_into_the_graph` |
| REQ-FRESH-064 | a snapshot whose `root` is not an absolute path (`.`, the empty text, a relative folder) says nothing about where the graph was built: built_here answers Unconfirmed whatever the current folder is, so the hooks and the query commands read such a graph as it is with `(wingbox-graph-unconfirmed ...)` and start no refresh in a folder nobody asked to map; an absolute root that is the folder still confirms it. | `tests/test_review_wp8_writes_install.py::ForeignGraphTest::test_a_snapshot_root_that_is_not_absolute_does_not_confirm_the_graph` |
| REQ-FRESH-065 | freshness.skipped_languages_line does not pass on the text of `.wingbox/graph-skipped.sexp`: it reads the language and the number of files of each `(skipped-language ...)` line and writes the fact again with the command Wingbox itself gives for that language, exactly as the builder writes it; a line with an unknown language, a number that is none, another command or free text gives no note, and a language is named once. | `tests/test_review_wp8_writes_install.py::ForeignGraphTest::test_skipped_language_notes_are_written_again_from_what_wingbox_knows` |
| REQ-FRESH-066 | a graph that was not built in its folder (a copied or committed `.wingbox/`, Unconfirmed) gives no skipped-language note at all, whatever its `graph-skipped.sexp` holds: not on the stderr of a query command, not in the context of the search hook, not at the head of an MCP answer; the note that the graph is unconfirmed stays. | `tests/test_review_wp8_writes_install.py::ForeignGraphTest::test_a_graph_that_was_not_built_here_gives_no_skipped_language_note` |
| REQ-FRESH-067 | `.wingbox/refresh.log` does not grow without bound: when a refresh starts and the log is larger than 1 MB, only its last part is kept (whole lines, less than a quarter of the limit) and the new refresh writes after it; a smaller log is left as it is. | `tests/test_review_wp8_writes_install.py::RefreshLogTest::test_the_refresh_log_keeps_its_last_part_under_a_fixed_size` |

## GO

| Requirement | Behaviour | Test |
|---|---|---|
| REQ-GO-001 | Go parsing preserves types, functions, receiver methods, constants, and imports. | `tests/test_go_language.py::GoLanguageTests::test_parser_extracts_go_types_functions_methods_and_imports` |
| REQ-GO-002 | Go local package imports resolve to every source file in the imported package boundary. | `tests/test_go_language.py::GoLanguageTests::test_graph_resolves_go_module_imports_to_package_files` |
| REQ-GO-003 | Go registration exposes canonical file, output, project, and LSP metadata. | `tests/test_go_language.py::GoLanguageTests::test_go_language_metadata_matches_gopls` |
| REQ-GO-004 | Go discovery excludes source owned by the standard vendor dependency tree. | `tests/test_go_language.py::GoLanguageTests::test_discovery_excludes_vendored_go_dependencies` |
| REQ-GO-005 | a Go file is a validation module when a struct field tag has the key validate or binding, or when it calls New of an imported github.com/go-playground/validator package; a comment, a string or the import alone gives no evidence. | `tests/test_validation_mark.py::OtherLanguagesValidationTest::test_go` |
| REQ-GO-006 | A Go file that calls a function another file of its package defines depends on that file. | `tests/test_review_wp6_other_langs.py::GoPackageFilesTest::test_a_file_depends_on_the_file_of_its_package_whose_function_it_calls` |
| REQ-GO-007 | A \_test.go file of the same directory and package depends on the file whose names it uses. | `tests/test_review_wp6_other_langs.py::GoPackageFilesTest::test_a_test_file_of_the_package_depends_on_the_file_it_tests` |
| REQ-GO-008 | Types, variables, constants and the receiver of a method link the files of a Go package. | `tests/test_review_wp6_other_langs.py::GoPackageFilesTest::test_types_variables_constants_and_receivers_link_the_files_of_a_package` |
| REQ-GO-009 | A name a Go file binds locally (a parameter, a variable, a type parameter, also the one a method receiver names), a struct field key, a name of another package or directory and a name two files define give no edge. | `tests/test_review_wp6_other_langs.py::GoPackageFilesTest::test_a_local_name_or_an_ambiguous_name_gives_no_edge` |
| REQ-GO-010 | A Go name used before a local declaration of the same name, or in another block, is the name of the package. | `tests/test_review_wp6_other_langs.py::GoPackageFilesTest::test_a_name_is_local_only_from_its_declaration_on` |
| REQ-GO-011 | The module line of go.mod is read with a trailing comment, with quotes and in the block form. | `tests/test_review_wp6_other_langs.py::GoModuleFileTest::test_a_comment_after_the_module_path_is_not_part_of_it` |
| REQ-GO-012 | A go.mod with a byte that is not UTF-8 gives a warning; the other modules are read and the graph is built. | `tests/test_review_wp6_other_langs.py::GoModuleFileTest::test_a_module_file_that_is_not_utf8_is_reported_and_the_build_goes_on` |
| REQ-GO-013 | A go.mod of 100 KB of blank lines, or of one long line, is read within a second. | `tests/test_review_wp6_other_langs.py::GoModuleFileTest::test_a_long_module_file_is_read_in_linear_time` |

## HTTP

| Requirement | Behaviour | Test |
|---|---|---|
| REQ-HTTP-001 | the transport has one endpoint, /mcp; any other path is answered 404 and never reaches the server. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::DecisionTest::test_only_the_mcp_endpoint_exists` |
| REQ-HTTP-002 | a request with an Origin header that is neither a loopback origin nor one given with --allow-origin is answered 403 (DNS rebinding); a request without Origin, as agents send it, passes. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::DecisionTest::test_a_foreign_origin_is_refused` |
| REQ-HTTP-003 | with a token set, a request passes only with `Authorization: Bearer <token>`; a missing, wrong or differently spelled credential is answered 401 with a WWW-Authenticate header, whatever the method. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::DecisionTest::test_a_request_without_the_token_is_refused` |
| REQ-HTTP-004 | the server opens no stream of its own and keeps no session, so GET and DELETE on the endpoint are answered 405 with `Allow: POST`. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::DecisionTest::test_get_and_delete_are_not_offered` |
| REQ-HTTP-005 | a body that is not JSON is answered 400 with a JSON-RPC parse error, and a batch (a JSON array) 400 with an invalid-request error. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::DecisionTest::test_a_body_that_is_not_one_message_is_refused` |
| REQ-HTTP-006 | an MCP-Protocol-Version header that names a revision the server does not speak is answered 400 with the list of supported revisions; a request without the header is served. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::DecisionTest::test_an_unknown_protocol_version_header_is_refused` |
| REQ-HTTP-007 | a request of revision 2026-07-28 must carry MCP-Protocol-Version and Mcp-Method equal to its body, and Mcp-Name equal to the tool of a tools/call; a missing or different header is answered 400 with error -32020. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::DecisionTest::test_the_headers_of_a_2026_request_must_match_its_body` |
| REQ-HTTP-008 | a notification is answered 202 without a body, a result 200, a message the server cannot accept 400, an unknown method 404 under revision 2026-07-28 and 200 with the JSON-RPC error under the handshake revisions. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::DecisionTest::test_the_status_follows_the_reply` |
| REQ-HTTP-009 | a body that is not declared as application/json is answered 415, an Accept header that allows neither JSON nor an event stream 406, a body over the size limit 413. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::DecisionTest::test_what_the_client_sends_and_accepts_is_checked` |
| REQ-HTTP-010 | without arguments the server listens on 127.0.0.1 only, for the current directory, with no token required. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::SettingsTest::test_the_default_is_this_machine_only` |
| REQ-HTTP-011 | listening on any address other than loopback is refused without a token; the token comes from WINGBOX_HTTP_TOKEN or from --token-file, is at least 32 visible ASCII characters, and cannot be given on the command line, where the process list would show it. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::SettingsTest::test_another_address_needs_a_token` |
| REQ-HTTP-012 | --root must be a directory, --port a port number, --tls-cert and --tls-key come together, and --allow-origin takes a scheme://host[:port] origin. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::SettingsTest::test_the_other_arguments_are_checked` |
| REQ-HTTP-013 | over HTTP the same server answers as over stdio: initialize, the initialized notification (202, no body), tools/list with every tool, then build and impact on a repository; a request without the token gets 401. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::ListenerTest::test_a_client_talks_to_the_real_server_over_http` |
| REQ-HTTP-014 | a tools/call that takes longer than the first wait is answered as an event stream when the client accepts one: keep-alive comment lines while the tool runs, then one `message` event with the reply; a quick call is answered as plain JSON. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::ListenerTest::test_a_long_call_is_answered_as_an_event_stream` |
| REQ-HTTP-015 | a client whose Accept header has no text/event-stream gets the reply of a long call as plain JSON. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::ListenerTest::test_a_client_that_takes_json_only_waits_for_json` |
| REQ-HTTP-016 | requests that arrive together reach the server one after another, as on stdio: the server keeps one loaded graph and its commands are not written for concurrent use. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::ListenerTest::test_one_call_runs_at_a_time` |
| REQ-HTTP-017 | a POST without Content-Length is answered 411 and one that announces more than the size limit 413, before the body is read. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::ListenerTest::test_a_request_without_a_length_or_over_the_limit_is_refused_unread` |
| REQ-HTTP-018 | with --tls-cert and --tls-key the listener speaks HTTPS: a client that trusts the certificate gets its answer, and plain HTTP on the same port gets none. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::ListenerTest::test_tls` |
| REQ-HTTP-019 | `wingbox mcp --http` runs the transport of the separate package wingbox-http with the remaining arguments, and without that package says how to install it and exits 2: the core package holds no listening code. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::CommandTest::test_wingbox_mcp_http_names_the_package_when_it_is_missing` |
| REQ-HTTP-020 | `wingbox-http --host 0.0.0.0` without a token exits 2 with the reason and opens no port. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::CommandTest::test_a_refused_start_explains_itself` |
| REQ-HTTP-021 | wingbox-http has the version of wingbox, requires exactly that version of it, and the `http` extra of wingbox pins exactly that version of wingbox-http. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::PackagingTest::test_the_versions_move_together` |
| REQ-HTTP-022 | the core package never imports wingbox_http at start: its name is used in one place, `_http_main` of the MCP server, which only `main` calls and only for `--http`; no other file of the core mentions the package. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::PackagingTest::test_the_core_loads_the_adapter_only_for_http` |
| REQ-HTTP-023 | a body of 200,000 opening brackets, which the JSON reader gives up on with a recursion error, is answered 400 with a JSON-RPC parse error like any other body that is not JSON. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::DecisionTest::test_a_body_nested_too_deeply_is_a_parse_error` |
| REQ-HTTP-024 | for an origin given with --allow-origin the transport answers the preflight of a browser (OPTIONS with Access-Control-Request-Method, which carries no token) with 204 and Access-Control-Allow-Methods POST and the request headers it reads, and every answer to that origin carries Access-Control-Allow-Origin with that origin and `Vary: Origin`; a loopback origin, which passes without the option, gets neither (its OPTIONS is answered 405, or 401 when a token is set), and a request without Origin gets none. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::DecisionTest::test_only_an_origin_given_with_allow_origin_gets_the_headers_a_browser_page_needs` |
| REQ-HTTP-025 | an argument the command does not take is refused without repeating what was typed after it: `--token <secret>`, `--token=<secret>` and a bare value give a message that names the option (`--token`) or says that an argument is not expected, and never the value, so the entry point, which prints the message, cannot log a token; the message for --token says where the token is read from. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::SettingsTest::test_the_value_of_an_unknown_option_is_never_repeated` |
| REQ-HTTP-026 | a Content-Length that is not a number in ASCII digits (the superscript two passes `isdigit()` and has no integer value; more digits than a number of the size limit) is answered 400, or 413 for a number over the limit, and a body nested too deeply for the JSON reader 400 with a parse error: each gets an HTTP answer instead of a dropped connection, and the listener answers the next request. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::ListenerTest::test_a_request_the_listener_cannot_read_is_answered_and_the_listener_goes_on` |
| REQ-HTTP-027 | the listener keeps at most `Limits.connections` connections open, each with one thread (64 by default): a connection beyond that gets no thread and is answered 503 at once, with or without a token; when a connection ends, the next one is served. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::ListenerTest::test_the_number_of_open_connections_is_capped` |
| REQ-HTTP-028 | the header and the body of one request must arrive within `Limits.request_seconds` in all (30 by default): a client that sends one byte at a time, each well within that time, is cut off when the total is over, in the header and in the body alike, so no client holds a thread for as long as it likes; a request that arrives in time is answered. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::ListenerTest::test_a_request_must_arrive_within_a_total_time` |
| REQ-HTTP-029 | at most `Limits.calls` calls run or wait for the one worker (16 by default): a call beyond that is answered 503 with a JSON-RPC error and Retry-After and never reaches the server; the calls that were taken are answered, and once they ended the next call is taken again. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::ListenerTest::test_the_number_of_waiting_calls_is_capped` |
| REQ-HTTP-030 | a call that still waits for its turn when its client closes the connection is taken out of the queue and never reaches the server, so abandoned calls do not make the next caller wait; the call that is running cannot be interrupted and ends; the log names the client that left. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::ListenerTest::test_a_waiting_call_whose_client_has_gone_is_not_run` |
| REQ-HTTP-031 | over a real connection, a page of an origin given with --allow-origin gets its preflight answered 204 with the Access-Control headers, without a token, and its POST answered with Access-Control-Allow-Origin naming that origin, also when the answer is a refusal (401) or an event stream; a page of a loopback origin gets its POST served but no Access-Control header on any answer, and its preflight refused, so its browser hands it nothing. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::ListenerTest::test_a_page_of_an_allowed_origin_can_read_the_answers` |
| REQ-HTTP-032 | `wingbox-http` opens its listener with exactly what the arguments, the environment and the token file say: the host and the port, the token as the access rule (and no token only on a loopback address), the certificate and key of --tls-cert and --tls-key, the origins of --allow-origin, and the protocol revisions of the MCP server; its first log line names the address and the project whose graph is served, and never the token. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::CommandTest::test_the_entry_point_listens_with_the_settings_it_was_given` |

## INSTALL

| Requirement | Behaviour | Test |
|---|---|---|
| REQ-INSTALL-001 | `wingbox install` must detect the assistants by their configuration folders and write, for each, the stamped skill, the always-on block and (Claude Code) the PreToolUse hook; assistants without a folder get nothing. | `tests/test_install.py::GlobalInstallTest::test_fresh_install_writes_skill_block_and_hook_for_detected_assistants` |

| REQ-INSTALL-003 | install must keep the text of CLAUDE.md and AGENTS.md outside its markers and every other setting and hook, back up settings.json once, add its hook once; uninstall must restore the files byte for byte and leave only the backup. | `tests/test_install.py::GlobalInstallTest::test_existing_files_and_other_hooks_are_kept_and_uninstall_restores_them` |
| REQ-INSTALL-004 | uninstall after an install into empty configuration folders must delete every file and folder install created, the record included. | `tests/test_install.py::GlobalInstallTest::test_uninstall_after_a_fresh_install_leaves_no_trace` |
| REQ-INSTALL-005 | `wingbox install --dry-run` must write nothing and name every file it would write or change. | `tests/test_install.py::GlobalInstallTest::test_dry_run_writes_nothing_and_names_every_file` |
| REQ-INSTALL-006 | `--assistant` must limit the install to the named assistants, even ones without a folder; an unknown name is a usage error. | `tests/test_install.py::GlobalInstallTest::test_assistant_option_limits_the_install_and_rejects_unknown_names` |
| REQ-INSTALL-007 | a wingbox skill folder without the install stamp (for example from `npx skills add`) must be neither overwritten nor removed. | `tests/test_install.py::GlobalInstallTest::test_a_skill_folder_written_by_another_tool_is_kept` |
| REQ-INSTALL-008 | a settings.json that is not a JSON object must not be rewritten; install reports the failure and exits 1. | `tests/test_install.py::GlobalInstallTest::test_invalid_settings_json_is_left_untouched` |
| REQ-INSTALL-009 | uninstalling Codex must keep \~/.agents/skills/wingbox while the shared `agents` target is still installed. | `tests/test_install.py::GlobalInstallTest::test_uninstalling_one_assistant_keeps_a_folder_another_one_uses` |
| REQ-INSTALL-010 | `wingbox install --project` (removed in 0.9.0) must exit non-zero, write nothing, and direct project setup to `wingbox init`, assistant setup to `wingbox install`, and legacy cleanup to `wingbox uninstall --project`. | `tests/test_install.py::ProjectInstallTest::test_project_install_is_refused_and_writes_nothing` |
| REQ-INSTALL-011 | `wingbox uninstall --project` must remove what `install --project` of an earlier version wrote (the stamped skill folders, the block, the hook entries, the folders it created, the record entry), keep a CLAUDE.md that links to AGENTS.md as a link, and restore AGENTS.md. | `tests/test_install.py::ProjectInstallTest::test_uninstall_project_removes_what_an_earlier_version_wrote` |
| REQ-INSTALL-012 | install must replace an outdated block between the markers in place, never add a second one, and keep the text before and after; unbalanced markers are left alone. | `tests/test_install.py::BlockEditTest::test_the_block_is_replaced_between_markers_and_nothing_else_changes` |
| REQ-INSTALL-013 | `wingbox install --sexp` must print one `(install-wrote <path> :what ... :assistant ...)` fact per target; the default output is the same facts as aligned text, with the home folder shown as \~. | `tests/test_install.py::InstallOutputTest::test_install_prints_sexp_with_sexp_and_a_pretty_print_by_default` |
| REQ-INSTALL-014 | an unknown option or assistant must exit with status 2 and a message on stderr. | `tests/test_install.py::InstallOutputTest::test_usage_errors_exit_with_2` |
| REQ-INSTALL-015 | the skill that install copies must be SKILL.md named wingbox with its references, and SKILL.md must describe `/wingbox` with no argument, with a file and with a command. | `tests/test_install.py::PackagedSkillTest::test_the_packaged_skill_holds_skill_md_references_and_the_slash_command` |
| REQ-INSTALL-016 | the pre-search hook on Grep must answer, as PreToolUse additionalContext, the modules and definitions that match the pattern with their direct dependents. | `tests/test_hook.py::HookOnABuiltGraphTest::test_a_grep_pattern_returns_matching_modules_and_definitions` |
| REQ-INSTALL-017 | a Grep pattern written for source lines (`def\s+order_total`) must be answered for its longest word when the pattern itself names nothing in the graph. | `tests/test_hook.py::HookOnABuiltGraphTest::test_a_pattern_of_code_falls_back_to_its_longest_word` |
| REQ-INSTALL-018 | the pre-search hook on Read must give the module of the file, its direct dependents and the `wingbox impact` hint; a file outside the graph gives nothing. | `tests/test_hook.py::HookOnABuiltGraphTest::test_a_read_names_the_dependents_of_the_file_and_the_impact_command` |
| REQ-INSTALL-019 | the hook must find the graph at .wingbox/index.sexp of the project root above the current folder; a graph that was not built there is read as it is, with a note to rebuild. | `tests/test_hook.py::HookOnABuiltGraphTest::test_the_graph_in_the_wingbox_folder_is_found` |
| REQ-INSTALL-020 | without a graph, on malformed input or an unknown tool, `wingbox hook pre-search` must print nothing on stdout and exit 0, so the tool call is never blocked. | `tests/test_hook.py::HookWithoutAGraphTest::test_no_graph_or_bad_input_gives_no_output_and_exit_0` |
| REQ-INSTALL-021 | the synthetic graph of the time-budget test must be a graph the query engine accepts, with the same dependents as the hook counts. | `tests/test_hook.py::HookBudgetTest::test_the_synthetic_graph_loads_in_the_query_engine` |
| REQ-INSTALL-022 | on a graph of 8,000 modules, 48,000 definitions and 96,000 edges the hook must answer in under one second of processor time and within 1,500 characters. | `tests/test_hook.py::HookBudgetTest::test_a_large_graph_is_answered_within_the_budget_and_the_size_cap` |
| REQ-INSTALL-023 | every hook entry Wingbox writes is in exec form on every system (`command` = the program, `args` = the rest), which Claude Code starts with no shell, so it runs on Windows without Git Bash and a path with spaces or backslashes needs no quoting; each entry carries `"onFailure": "continue"`, so a missing program or a timeout never blocks Grep, Glob, Read or an edit; the shell form of older versions is still recognised as Wingbox's own entry. | `tests/test_portable.py::HookFormTest::test_every_hook_is_written_in_exec_form_and_never_blocks` |
| REQ-INSTALL-024 | the Grep hint uses smart case, as the command line matches without case: a pattern with no upper-case letter (escapes such as \\s do not count) matches without case, a pattern with an upper-case letter matches with case, and the tool's case-insensitive flag (-i) always matches without case. | `tests/test_hook.py::HookOnABuiltGraphTest::test_a_grep_pattern_matches_with_smart_case` |
| REQ-INSTALL-025 | in a source repository (a git work tree or a folder with a project manifest, holding files of a supported language) without .wingbox/index.sexp, the search hook answers once per session and repository with `(wingbox-no-graph :root <root> :hint ...)` naming `wingbox build`; the next call of the same session says nothing, another session is told again; the marker lives under \~/.wingbox/hook-hints, nothing is written into the repository and no graph is built. | `tests/test_hook.py::HookWithoutAGraphHintTest::test_a_source_repository_without_a_graph_is_named_once_per_session` |
| REQ-INSTALL-026 | no hint in a folder that is no repository (no .git, no project manifest), in a repository without a source file of a supported language (only documents), or in the home folder itself even when it is a git work tree; old markers are pruned. | `tests/test_hook.py::HookWithoutAGraphHintTest::test_no_hint_outside_a_source_repository` |
| REQ-INSTALL-027 | the hook knows a source file by the extensions the builder parses, and the check without a graph stays under 100 ms in a repository of 5,000 files. | `tests/test_hook.py::HookWithoutAGraphHintTest::test_the_extensions_are_the_builders_and_the_check_is_fast` |
| REQ-INSTALL-030 | the install list names, per detected assistant, only items in the home folder: the skill, the instructions block (with its file), and for Claude Code the search, edit, session-start and git hooks in \~/.claude/settings.json; all ticked by default. | `tests/test_install_list.py::ItemsTest::test_the_list_holds_the_global_items_of_each_assistant` |
| REQ-INSTALL-031 | `--without` takes ITEM or ASSISTANT:ITEM, comma separated or repeated (skill, block, search-hook, edit-hook, session-hook, git-hook); `none` leaves nothing out; an unknown item or assistant is a usage error that names the known ones. | `tests/test_install_list.py::ItemsTest::test_without_names_an_item_or_an_item_of_one_assistant` |
| REQ-INSTALL-032 | `wingbox install --without git-hook,codex:block` writes every other item and not these, reports them as install-left-out, records the choice in \~/.wingbox/installed.sexp; a later install without --without keeps the choice; `--without none` puts everything back. | `tests/test_install_list.py::WithoutTest::test_left_out_items_are_not_written_and_the_choice_is_kept` |
| REQ-INSTALL-033 | installing with --without search-hook over a full install removes that hook entry and keeps the others; with every hook left out no Wingbox entry stays in settings.json. | `tests/test_install_list.py::WithoutTest::test_unticking_an_installed_hook_removes_it` |
| REQ-INSTALL-034 | `wingbox uninstall --without skill,session-hook` removes everything else install wrote and keeps the skills and the session-start hook. | `tests/test_install_list.py::WithoutTest::test_uninstall_keeps_the_items_named_by_without` |
| REQ-INSTALL-035 | `wingbox update` refreshes a global install with the recorded --without, so an item the person left out is never added back; it refreshes no project install, and says once per recorded project install that its files are still there and that `wingbox uninstall --project` in that folder removes them. | `tests/test_install_list.py::WithoutTest::test_update_refreshes_only_the_installed_items` |
| REQ-INSTALL-036 | without single-key input the list is numbered: typing numbers toggles those items (a second time ticks again), Enter installs the list as shown, q quits and installs nothing; a word that is not a number of the list is named. | `tests/test_install_list.py::TerminalTest::test_the_numbered_list_toggles_items_and_quits` |
| REQ-INSTALL-037 | install asks only a person at a terminal (stdin and stdout are terminals, no CI, no agent variable, no --yes, --dry-run or --sexp); `wingbox install` with stdin from a pipe asks nothing and installs every item. | `tests/test_install_list.py::TerminalTest::test_nothing_is_asked_outside_a_terminal` |
| REQ-INSTALL-038 | `wingbox install --dry-run --without git-hook` prints the same list with the git hook unticked and writes nothing. | `tests/test_install_list.py::TerminalTest::test_dry_run_prints_the_list_and_writes_nothing` |
| REQ-INSTALL-039 | in a terminal `wingbox install` shows the list; arrow down and space untick the item under the cursor, Enter installs the rest and records the choice; q installs nothing. | `tests/test_install_list.py::TerminalTest::test_a_person_unticks_items_with_the_arrow_keys` |
| REQ-INSTALL-040 | a Grep, Glob or Read call made from a script (its tool_use_id starts with `repl_`) gets no context from the search hook, and the hook neither looks for the graph, nor checks or refreshes it, nor reads it: a script may make hundreds of calls, and each context block lands in the model's context. | `tests/test_hook_code_calls.py::CallFromCodeTest::test_a_call_made_from_a_script_gets_no_context_and_costs_no_graph_work` |
| REQ-INSTALL-041 | a call the model makes itself (tool_use_id `toolu_...`), a call with no tool_use_id and a call whose id has any other form get the context as before: only the observed mark of a call from code switches it off. | `tests/test_hook_code_calls.py::CallFromCodeTest::test_a_direct_call_an_absent_id_and_an_unknown_id_keep_the_context` |
| REQ-INSTALL-042 | when the search hook has not answered within its own time budget (well under the 10 s Claude Code gives it), the process ends with exit code 0 and nothing on stdout: an unfinished hook refuses a tool call made from code, a silent one lets it run. | `tests/test_hook_code_calls.py::TimeBudgetTest::test_a_hook_that_cannot_answer_in_time_gives_up_silently` |
| REQ-INSTALL-043 | an answer that is ready within the budget is printed whole, and the process does not wait for the budget to pass. | `tests/test_hook_code_calls.py::TimeBudgetTest::test_an_answer_within_the_budget_is_printed_whole` |
| REQ-INSTALL-044 | the always-on block tells the assistant to ask the graph before `grep`, `rg`, `find` or opening files and to read only what it names, with text search as the stated fallback; to run `impact` before changing a file; to take the tests of a change from the dependents `impact` names and to show for every changed function a test that reaches it, which proves reach and not the strength of the assertions. Every command it names is a command of `wingbox`, and the block stays short: at most 1,600 characters. | `tests/test_install.py::BlockTextTest::test_the_block_puts_the_graph_before_text_search_and_ties_tests_to_impact` |
| REQ-INSTALL-045 | SKILL.md has a short section for use from code with the one call a script needs (`wingbox impact <files> 2 --json`) and how to read it, and says that each row carries the reason it is listed; the always-on block says the same in one line. Neither gives a benchmark number or tells the agent not to search, and the skill names `--sexp` only for the commands that talk to a person (init, learn and the setup commands) and where it says what the option still prints. | `tests/test_output_formats_cli.py::SkillTextTest::test_the_skill_and_the_block_say_how_a_script_reads_an_answer` |
| REQ-INSTALL-046 | references/code-mode.md, the text for Claude Code's Code Mode, gives for each question an instruction first (what one call returns and that the value of the block is the answer) and a short example whose last expression is that value (no example ends with an assignment); for `impact` it states the three facts of the answer: each dependent once and never the file itself, the list not cut, read from every source file of the graph with test files included. Every command the text calls (`impact` for several files, `usages`, `quick`, `diff_functions`, `boundaries`) answers on a real project with the keys the text reads, the item a `diff-fn` fact names is accepted by `usages`, and the test files of the change come out of the text's own pattern. The skill and the always-on block point to the file and say the same in one line, and the plugin copies are the same text. | `tests/test_json_errors.py::CodeModeRecipesTest::test_every_call_of_the_recipes_answers_with_the_keys_the_recipes_read` |
| REQ-INSTALL-047 | when `wingbox hook pre-search` returns, with an answer, without one or after an error, the thread of its time limit has ended: no thread but the caller's is alive, so no daemon thread runs while the interpreter shuts down (the hook process ended with a segmentation fault on macOS when one did). The limit itself still ends a hook that runs over it. | `tests/test_hook.py::HookTimerTest::test_the_timer_thread_has_ended_when_the_hook_returns` |
| REQ-INSTALL-048 | install --global-gitignore appends .wingbox/ once to Git's configured global excludes file or its XDG default. Plain install and dry run leave it alone. | `tests/test_install.py::GlobalInstallTest::test_global_gitignore_is_opt_in_and_respects_git_configuration` |
| REQ-INSTALL-049 | the terminal shows a Global Git ignore checkbox, unchecked unless --global-gitignore is passed. Toggling it controls writing the global excludes and does not save an assistant --without choice. | `tests/test_install_list.py::TerminalTest::test_the_global_gitignore_checkbox_is_opt_in_and_can_be_toggled` |
| REQ-INSTALL-050 | when a settings file or an instructions file cannot be read or written (not UTF-8, a folder in its place, read-only), `wingbox install` raises nothing: it answers `(install-failed <file> :what hook\|block ... :reason ...)` for that file, puts the other items in place, writes \~/.wingbox/installed.sexp so that `wingbox uninstall` knows what to remove, and its status is 1; the file it could not use is left as it was and no backup of it is left behind. `wingbox uninstall` then removes what was installed and keeps the file with the reason, also without raising. | `tests/test_review_wp8_writes_install.py::InstallFailureTest::test_install_reports_a_file_it_cannot_use_and_records_what_it_wrote` |
| REQ-INSTALL-051 | `wingbox install --yes --sexp` and `wingbox uninstall --sexp` with a settings file that is a folder, and with a record \~/.wingbox/installed.sexp that cannot be read, print the failure as a fact (`install-failed`, `uninstall-kept` or `uninstall-failed`, with the file and the reason) and no traceback; install exits 1 and nothing is written before a record that cannot be read. | `tests/test_review_wp8_writes_install.py::InstallFailureTest::test_the_command_prints_the_failure_as_a_fact_and_no_traceback` |
| REQ-INSTALL-052 | the command the search hook suggests for a file, `wingbox impact <path>`, carries the path quoted for a shell when it holds a character a shell reads (a space, `;`, `$(...)`, a quote, a backquote, `&`, `\|`): split as a shell splits it, the command is `wingbox`, `impact` and the path, nothing else; the hint stays one S-expression string. A plain path is written as before, without quotes. | `tests/test_review_wp8_writes_install.py::HookHintTest::test_the_hint_quotes_a_path_with_shell_characters` |
