| REQ-RUST-043 | a Rust file with a builder chain of 3,000 calls, an expression of 3,000 terms, 3,000 `else if` branches, 3,000 nested groups in the arguments of a macro or a path of 3,000 segments does not stop the build: its `use` declarations, the struct defined after the long part with the method of its `impl`, and every method call of the chain are read as in any file. | `tests/test_review_wp5_py_ts_rust.py::LongTreeOtherLanguageTests::test_rust_reads_a_file_with_long_chains` |
| REQ-RUST-044 | a crate name in code is the package that the manifest of the importing package takes from a folder of the tree under that name (`utils = { path = "../utils" }`, or `utils.workspace = true` with a path in `[workspace.dependencies]`): with two packages named `utils`, each service reaches its own; a name the manifest takes from a registry (`log = "0.4"`) is no local package, also when the tree holds a package of that name; a name the manifest does not state is the one local package of that name, and none when two share it. | `tests/test_review_wp5_py_ts_rust.py::RustResolutionTests::test_a_crate_name_is_read_from_the_manifest_of_the_importing_package` |
| REQ-RUST-045 | `include!("file.rs")` with one literal path is a dependency of the including file on that file, relative to its own folder, with the line of the macro; a path built with `concat!` or `env!`, and a file that is not in the graph, give none. | `tests/test_review_wp5_py_ts_rust.py::RustResolutionTests::test_an_included_file_is_a_dependency` |
| REQ-RUST-046 | the search for `#[path = ".."]` attributes and for `extern crate a as b;` reads 100 KB of `#[` without a closing bracket, of empty lines or of blanks in well under half a minute, where the first took over a minute and the second hours; a path attribute, also inside `cfg_attr` and an inline module, and an `extern crate` alias after the long part are still found, and `path = "p"` outside an attribute starts no reading of the file. | `tests/test_review_wp5_py_ts_rust.py::RustResolutionTests::test_the_expressions_of_the_rust_reader_read_a_long_line_once` |
| REQ-RUST-047 | `Self::member` inside an impl block of a type names a member another file's impl block of that type defines: plan.rs (`Self::LIMIT`) depends on seed.rs; a member the file of the type defines adds no edge (`Self::defaults()`). | `tests/test_rust_type_members.py::RustTypeMemberTest::test_self_in_an_impl_block_names_the_members_of_the_other_impl_blocks` |
| REQ-RUST-048 | a member the file of the type defines, a method called on a value (`r.plan()`), an enum variant, a trait impl in another file, a function of the same name as a member and a module function give no edge to an impl file. | `tests/test_rust_type_members.py::RustTypeMemberTest::test_what_needs_the_type_of_a_value_or_a_trait_gives_no_edge` |

## SEC

| Requirement | Behaviour | Test |
|---|---|---|
| REQ-SEC-001 | the builder, the query commands and the `wingbox` command (`scripts/`, `src/`) and the tools of the repository (`tools/`) must not import a module that opens network connections, however the import is written; the README states that nothing leaves the machine. | `tests/test_local_only.py::LocalOnlyTest::test_the_package_imports_no_network_module` |
| REQ-SEC-002 | map.html must be one self-contained file: no script, style, font or data loaded from a URL, so that it opens offline. | `tests/test_local_only.py::LocalOnlyTest::test_the_map_page_loads_nothing_from_outside` |
| REQ-SEC-004 | a build that meets files of a language whose parser is missing, and a query after it, finish without loading urllib.request, http.client or ssl: the missing parser is reported with the pip extra that installs it, never fetched. | `tests/test_local_only.py::MissingParserTest::test_a_build_that_skips_a_language_loads_no_download_code` |
| REQ-SEC-006 | no source file of Wingbox starts a Python with -m or -c without -P before it (or -I, which includes it): `python -m` and `python -c` put the starting folder first on the module search path, and Wingbox is started inside the project it reads. The commands are built by wingbox/launch.py alone. | `tests/test_project_code_never_runs.py::ProjectCodeNeverRunsTest::test_every_python_wingbox_starts_leaves_the_starting_folder_out` |
| REQ-SEC-007 | the refresh the hooks start in the project folder (`python -P -m wingbox.refresh`) imports the installed Wingbox: a project that holds wingbox/, pip/, yaml.py, json.py or tree_sitter.py gets none of them run, and the refresh itself runs (its log holds a line of the real refresh). | `tests/test_project_code_never_runs.py::ProjectCodeNeverRunsTest::test_a_refresh_started_in_a_project_runs_no_file_of_the_project` |
| REQ-SEC-008 | `python -P -m wingbox` (the form of the hooks of an install without the wingbox program, and of the first build of the MCP server) started in a project that holds files named like the modules Wingbox imports builds the graph of that project and runs none of them; they are in the graph as source files. | `tests/test_project_code_never_runs.py::ProjectCodeNeverRunsTest::test_the_command_started_as_a_module_runs_no_file_of_the_project` |
| REQ-SEC-009 | a command a person starts as `python -m wingbox` takes the starting folder off its module search path before it loads anything else: the entry of that folder and the empty entry go, every other entry stays in order; the folder stays when the Wingbox package itself lies in it. A project file named yaml.py is then not run by a build started without -P. | `tests/test_project_code_never_runs.py::ProjectCodeNeverRunsTest::test_the_starting_folder_comes_off_the_path_of_a_command` |
| REQ-SEC-010 | the HTTP transport lives in its own distribution, outside the folders REQ-SEC-001 scans, and only listens: its code imports no HTTP client and opens no outbound connection. | `tests/test_http_transport.py::PackagingTest::test_the_adapter_only_listens` |
| REQ-SEC-011 | `wingbox add` in every form, --list included, run in a project with a temporary home folder that holds a parser folder of 0.12: creates, changes and removes no file in the home folder or in the project, and finishes without loading urllib.request, http.client, ssl, ftplib or smtplib. | `tests/test_add_command.py::AddCommandTest::test_the_command_writes_nothing_and_loads_no_network_module` |
| REQ-SEC-012 | `wingbox build`, `wingbox impact --diff` and `wingbox diff_functions` in a repository whose own Git configuration names programs start none of them: `core.fsmonitor`, a clean and a smudge filter that `.gitattributes` applies (with `required = true`), a filter process, `diff.external`, a textconv driver and a `post-index-change` hook under `.git/hooks`. The commands still answer: the build names the commit of a clean tree and both diff commands name the changed function. Plain `git status` and `git diff` in the same repository start every one of these programs (checked where the test runs on POSIX; on Windows the programs are written the same way and only their absence is asserted). | `tests/test_review_wp1_git_diff.py::GitProgramsNeverRunTest::test_programs_of_the_repository_configuration_are_not_started` |
| REQ-SEC-013 | in a repository with a submodule whose own Git configuration sets `core.fsmonitor` to a program, `wingbox build`, `wingbox impact --diff` and `wingbox diff_functions` do not start that program (Git is not run inside the submodule), and the change of the repository itself is still named. Plain `git status` starts it (checked on POSIX). | `tests/test_review_wp1_git_diff.py::GitProgramsNeverRunTest::test_git_is_not_started_inside_a_submodule` |
| REQ-SEC-014 | in a repository whose configuration declares a promisor remote (a partial clone) with a program as its transport, objects that are missing are not fetched: `wingbox build` finishes and `wingbox impact --diff <ref>` answers `(error git-diff-failed ...)` with exit status 1, and the program is not started. Plain `git diff <ref>` starts it (checked on POSIX). | `tests/test_review_wp1_git_diff.py::GitProgramsNeverRunTest::test_missing_objects_are_not_fetched` |
| REQ-SEC-015 | a filter driver of the repository's configuration whose name holds `=` cannot be overridden on Git's command line, so Git is not started on the work tree at all: `git_safe.run` raises `GitRefused` for `status` and `diff`, `wingbox build` finishes and names no commit, `wingbox impact --diff` answers `(error git-diff-failed ...)` that names the driver, with exit status 1, and the program is not started. Plain `git status` starts it (checked on POSIX). | `tests/test_review_wp1_git_diff.py::GitProgramsNeverRunTest::test_a_filter_that_cannot_be_switched_off_stops_the_call` |
| REQ-SEC-016 | `scripts/git_descendants.py <ref>` refuses a ref that starts with `-` with a message on standard error and exit status 1 before Git is started, and lists the descendants of a commit without starting the signature program of the repository's configuration (`log.showSignature` with `gpg.program`) for a signed descendant. Plain `git log` starts it (checked on POSIX). | `tests/test_review_wp1_git_diff.py::GitProgramsNeverRunTest::test_the_descendants_script_refuses_an_option_and_starts_no_program` |
| REQ-SEC-017 | a TypeScript member name that holds line breaks and the text of a fact adds no fact to the graph: no line of `.wingbox/index.sexp` starts with the forged text, the graph loads, `impact` of the file the forged fact names lists no dependent, and `analyze` of the file with the name answers. | `tests/test_review_wp7_builder.py::NoForgedFactTest::test_a_member_name_with_a_line_break_adds_no_fact` |
| REQ-SEC-018 | whatever control character or line separator (line feed, carriage return, vertical tab, NEL, U+2028, U+2029, NUL) stands in a module path, a purpose, an entity name, a base, a decorator, a method or a variable name, the graph file and the use file hold one fact per line with the character written as an escape, and the reader loads the graph with every node. | `tests/test_review_wp7_builder.py::NoForgedFactTest::test_no_name_puts_a_control_character_into_a_written_line` |
| REQ-SEC-019 | a source file whose name holds a line break is left out of the graph with a notice and a `(not-parsed-file ... :reason file-name)` fact that stays on one line; the graph holds no forged line and answers for the other files; a question about the file answers `not-in-graph ... :reason file-name`; and such a file created later is not patched into the graph. | `tests/test_review_wp7_builder.py::NoForgedFactTest::test_a_file_whose_name_holds_a_line_break_is_left_out_with_a_notice` |
| REQ-SEC-020 | a chart name or a dependency name of a Chart.yaml, a workload name, an image, a label or a namespace that holds a line break, a bracket or another control character adds no line to the output of `wingbox infra`: with `--append` every appended line is an infra fact, a comment or empty, IDs and edge ends that are no plain atoms are quoted, and the graph still loads. | `tests/test_review_wp7_builder.py::InfraTopologyTest::test_a_name_with_a_line_break_adds_no_fact_to_the_graph` |
| REQ-SEC-021 | the check of REQ-SEC-001 reads the imports with `ast`, so it names a network module however it is imported: second in `import os, socket`, as `from urllib import request`, `import urllib.request as r`, `import socketserver`, `from http import client`, inside a function or under `if`, and through `__import__("socket")` or `importlib.import_module("http.client")`; `urllib.parse`, a relative import and a module whose name only starts like one (`sockets`, `http_helpers`) pass. | `tests/test_local_only.py::LocalOnlyTest::test_no_way_of_writing_a_network_import_escapes_the_check` |

## TS-ALIAS

| Requirement | Behaviour | Test |
|---|---|---|
| REQ-TS-ALIAS-001 | A "//" inside a tsconfig string, such as a \$schema URL, is not a comment, so the paths of that tsconfig still resolve. | `tests/test_typescript_language.py::TypeScriptResolverTests::test_url_in_a_tsconfig_string_keeps_its_path_aliases` |
| REQ-TS-ALIAS-002 | Line comments, block comments and trailing commas of a tsconfig are ignored, so its paths still resolve. | `tests/test_typescript_language.py::TypeScriptResolverTests::test_tsconfig_comments_and_trailing_commas_are_ignored` |
| REQ-TS-ALIAS-003 | A tsconfig that only extends a base file inherits its paths, and the inherited paths resolve relative to the file that declares them. | `tests/test_typescript_language.py::TypeScriptResolverTests::test_paths_inherited_through_extends_resolve_from_the_declaring_file` |
| REQ-TS-ALIAS-004 | Through a chain or a list of extends the nearest paths and the nearest baseUrl win, and paths resolve against the effective baseUrl. | `tests/test_typescript_language.py::TypeScriptResolverTests::test_extends_chain_and_list_let_the_nearest_declaration_win` |
| REQ-TS-ALIAS-005 | A circular extends chain ends at the repeated file; the paths declared by the tsconfig itself still resolve. | `tests/test_typescript_language.py::TypeScriptResolverTests::test_circular_extends_keeps_the_paths_of_the_tsconfig_itself` |
| REQ-TS-ALIAS-006 | A tsconfig saved with a UTF-8 byte order mark is read, so its paths still resolve. | `tests/test_typescript_language.py::TypeScriptResolverTests::test_tsconfig_saved_with_a_byte_order_mark_is_read` |
| REQ-TS-ALIAS-007 | the path aliases of a `jsconfig.json` and of a project file that a `references` entry of `tsconfig.json` names (`tsconfig.app.json`, or a folder with its own `tsconfig.json`) resolve for the files below that project file, like the aliases of a `tsconfig.json`. | `tests/test_review_wp5_py_ts_rust.py::TypeScriptReaderTests::test_aliases_of_jsconfig_and_of_a_referenced_tsconfig` |
| REQ-TS-ALIAS-008 | a tsconfig named by `extends` is read only when the file it leads to lies inside the repository: an absolute path, a relative path that climbs out and a link that leads out are refused with a notice and give no alias, while an `extends` inside the repository is applied as before. | `tests/test_review_wp5_py_ts_rust.py::TypeScriptReaderTests::test_a_project_file_outside_the_repository_is_not_read` |

## TS-BASEURL

| Requirement | Behaviour | Test |
|---|---|---|
| REQ-TS-BASEURL-001 | with `baseUrl` in the owning tsconfig, a bare specifier that no `paths` entry matches is looked up below baseUrl, as TypeScript does (thingsboard ui-ngx: `src/app/core/http/dashboard-report.service` from public-api.ts); `paths` entries still win, a tsconfig with baseUrl and no paths works the same, and a specifier that names no file there stays a package import. | `tests/test_typescript_base_url.py::BaseUrlTest::test_an_import_written_from_base_url_resolves_with_and_without_paths` |

| Requirement | Behaviour | Test |

| REQ-TS-JS-005 | Minified files (.min.js or a line over 5,000 characters), bundles (bundle in the name) and JavaScript under node_modules, dist, build or vendor are not discovered; written JavaScript next to them is. | `tests/test_typescript_language.py::TypeScriptResolverTests::test_generated_javascript_is_not_a_module` |
| REQ-TS-JS-006 | .mts and .cts files are TypeScript modules (declaration files .d.mts and .d.cts are not); an import of ./x.mjs or ./x.cjs names x.mts or x.cts when it exists, as TypeScript resolves it. | `tests/test_typescript_language.py::TypeScriptResolverTests::test_mts_and_cts_files_are_modules_named_by_mjs_and_cjs_imports` |
| REQ-TS-JS-007 | a build says how many JavaScript files it left out because of a line over 5,000 characters and names them: a written file with one long data URI is not in the graph, and the notice is where its name appears; a build that leaves no file out prints no such line. | `tests/test_review_wp5_py_ts_rust.py::TypeScriptReaderTests::test_the_build_names_the_files_left_out_as_minified` |

## TS-SYM

| Requirement | Behaviour | Test |
|---|---|---|
| REQ-TS-SYM-001 | a named, aliased or default import of S through barrels (`export * from`, `export { X as Y } from`, `export * as ns from`) references S in its defining file; the barrels that name S reference it too; module dependencies (impact) do not change. | `tests/test_symbol_usages.py::NameResolutionTests::test_typescript_imports_follow_reexport_chains_to_the_definition` |
| REQ-TS-SYM-002 | `ns.S` after `import * as ns`, `<ns.Comp />` and `ns.Enum.Member` are uses of S, Comp and Enum; a parameter or local variable named like the namespace shadows it and its members are not uses. | `tests/test_symbol_usages.py::UseSiteTests::test_typescript_namespace_members_and_shadowing` |
| REQ-TS-SYM-003 | a TypeScript file that imports S is a user of S (the import names it); a file whose only S is its own declaration, parameter or type parameter is not. | `tests/test_symbol_usages.py::UseSiteTests::test_typescript_local_names_shadow_imported_names` |
| REQ-TS-SYM-004 | a TypeScript `const` or `let` in a block shadows an imported name only inside that block; the same name elsewhere in the function is a use. A `var` shadows it in the whole function. | `tests/test_usages_command.py::UsagesCommandTests::test_typescript_block_scoped_names_shadow_only_their_block` |
| REQ-TS-SYM-005 | in TypeScript `usages <file>::Class.method` lists a call of a static method through the class name (`Database.open()`), a call on a module-level instance (`export const db = new Database()` or `const typed: Database = make()`, then `db.query()`) through a barrel of `export *`, and a member of an enum (`Mode.Fast`). | `tests/test_review_wp5_py_ts_rust.py::MemberUseTests::test_typescript_class_and_instance_members` |
| REQ-TS-SYM-006 | a member of a class, an object or an enum is named in the graph only when its key is an identifier, a number or a string written on one line without an escape; a computed key (a template literal with a line break that spells a fact of the graph) names nothing, so that no line of the graph comes from the text of a key. | `tests/test_review_wp5_py_ts_rust.py::TypeScriptReaderTests::test_a_computed_key_is_not_a_member_name` |

## UPDATE

| Requirement | Behaviour | Test |
|---|---|---|
| REQ-UPDATE-001 | `wingbox update` must choose pipx when `pipx list --short` names wingbox and Wingbox runs from pipx's wingbox environment, uv likewise with `uv tool list` and `uv tool dir`, and pip of the running Python otherwise, also when another installer holds a second copy. | `tests/test_update.py::MethodDetectionTest::test_the_installer_that_lists_wingbox_is_chosen` |
| REQ-UPDATE-002 | in a Docker image or an editable or local install, `wingbox update` must change nothing, exit 1 and name the command to use instead. | `tests/test_update.py::MethodDetectionTest::test_docker_and_source_installs_say_what_to_run_instead` |
| REQ-UPDATE-003 | `wingbox update --check` must report the installed and the latest version through the installer and install nothing. | `tests/test_update.py::CheckTest::test_check_reports_the_latest_version_without_installing` |
| REQ-UPDATE-004 | when the installer cannot reach the index, `--check` must end with an `update-failed` fact that quotes the installer and exit 1, without a traceback. | `tests/test_update.py::CheckTest::test_check_without_internet_fails_clearly` |
| REQ-UPDATE-005 | `wingbox update --dry-run` must print the upgrade command and the installs it would refresh, and not run the upgrade. | `tests/test_update.py::UpgradeTest::test_dry_run_runs_no_installer_command_that_changes_anything` |
| REQ-UPDATE-006 | when the installer's upgrade fails, `wingbox update` must report it, exit 1 and refresh nothing. | `tests/test_update.py::UpgradeTest::test_a_failed_upgrade_refreshes_nothing` |
| REQ-UPDATE-007 | after a successful upgrade, `wingbox update` must run `wingbox install` of the new version for every recorded install, so an outdated skill is rewritten, and report `update-from`, `update-to`, `update-method` and `update-refreshed`. | `tests/test_update.py::UpgradeTest::test_after_an_upgrade_the_installed_skills_are_refreshed` |
| REQ-UPDATE-008 | `wingbox update` must choose pipx when `pipx list --short` names wingbox but exits 1 because another package has a missing interpreter, as pipx does. | `tests/test_update.py::MethodDetectionTest::test_pipx_is_chosen_when_another_pipx_package_is_broken` |
| REQ-UPDATE-009 | without old-layout files `wingbox update` must print no warning; `--check` runs the same check. | `tests/test_update.py::OldLayoutWarningTest::test_a_clean_project_gives_no_warning` |
| REQ-UPDATE-010 | in an environment without pip (made by `uv venv`), `wingbox update` upgrades through `uv pip install --python <this interpreter> --upgrade wingbox` when uv is on PATH, reads the latest version from `uv pip install --dry-run`, and without uv says what to run instead of failing on "No module named pip". | `tests/test_update.py::NoPipTest::test_an_environment_without_pip_updates_through_uv_pip` |
| REQ-UPDATE-011 | an environment made with `uv venv` has no pip module, and `update.has_pip` of its interpreter says so; the running interpreter of the tests has its own answer from the same function. | `tests/test_update.py::NoPipTest::test_a_real_uv_venv_has_no_pip_and_is_detected` |
| REQ-UPDATE-012 | inside a Homebrew keg (the environment folder lies under Cellar/wingbox/&lt;version>/), `wingbox update` must change nothing, run no installer, exit 1 and name `brew upgrade wingbox`, also when uv is on PATH and the environment has no pip; an environment outside a keg is chosen as before. | `tests/test_update.py::MethodDetectionTest::test_a_homebrew_install_is_upgraded_by_homebrew` |
| REQ-UPDATE-013 | on Windows `wingbox update` must name the wingbox.exe it was started through from sys.argv[0], which a console-script launcher sets to its own path with or without the `.exe` ending, and when that file is a link (pipx links the program into its bin folder) also the file it points at; started as `python -m wingbox.cli`, through a file of another name, through a path that does not exist, or on another system it names none. | `tests/test_update.py::RunningLauncherTest::test_the_launcher_is_named_only_on_windows_and_only_for_wingbox_exe` |
| REQ-UPDATE-014 | while the installer's upgrade runs, the path of the running wingbox.exe must be free (the launcher is renamed beside itself, which Windows allows for a running program), so the installer writes the new launcher there; the old file is then handed to the removal that runs once the command has ended, and the upgrade's own result is returned unchanged. | `tests/test_update.py::UpgradePastLauncherTest::test_the_installer_finds_the_launcher_path_free_and_the_old_file_goes` |
| REQ-UPDATE-015 | when the installer wrote no new launcher (nothing to upgrade, or the upgrade failed), `wingbox update` must rename the old wingbox.exe back, leave no other file beside it, start no removal and return the installer's result; without a launcher to move (another system, `python -m wingbox.cli`) the installer runs as it is. | `tests/test_update.py::UpgradePastLauncherTest::test_without_a_new_launcher_the_old_one_is_back` |
| REQ-UPDATE-016 | `wingbox update` must print what the launcher step reports: `(update-left <file> :reason ...)` when the removal of the old launcher could not be started, after which the upgrade still counts as done and the installs are refreshed. | `tests/test_update.py::UpgradePastLauncherTest::test_what_the_launcher_step_reports_is_part_of_the_answer` |
| REQ-UPDATE-017 | the removal started by `wingbox update` must delete the old launcher once no process holds it: on Windows the file stays while a handle on it is open and is gone soon after the handle is closed; elsewhere it is gone at once. | `tests/test_update.py::UpgradePastLauncherTest::test_the_old_launcher_is_removed_once_it_is_free` |
| REQ-UPDATE-018 | started the way a launcher starts Python (the launcher file itself run as a zip archive, first on sys.path) and after the installed version was read through importlib.metadata, which keeps that archive open, `wingbox update` must still rename the launcher: the installer writes the new one in its place, nothing is reported about the launcher, and the old file is gone soon after the command has ended. On Windows an open file cannot be renamed, so the handle has to be closed first. | `tests/test_update.py::LauncherArchiveTest::test_the_launcher_python_runs_as_an_archive_is_renamed_and_replaced` |
| REQ-UPDATE-019 | when the wingbox.exe on PATH is a link to the launcher inside the environment (pipx, where Windows allows links), both paths must be free while the installer runs: the installer writes the new launcher and the new link, both old files are handed to the removal, and when the installer writes neither, both are renamed back and the link still leads to the launcher. | `tests/test_update.py::UpgradePastLauncherTest::test_a_linked_launcher_and_the_file_it_points_at_are_both_replaced` |
| REQ-UPDATE-020 | `wingbox update` must look in the current folder and its parents up to the project root and in every --project install of \~/.wingbox/installed.sexp for .code_graph.sexp, .code_graph.uses.sexp, .code_graph.uses-cache.json, .code_graph_cache.json and wingbox-out/, print for each project one `! WARNING: <project>: old Wingbox files left: <names> - Wingbox now uses .wingbox/ only; remove them yourself` line (yellow on a terminal without NO_COLOR, plain otherwise), the fact `(update-warning <project> :old-files (...))` with --sexp, and delete nothing. | `tests/test_update.py::OldLayoutWarningTest::test_old_files_are_named_in_yellow_or_plain_and_kept` |

## ZONE

| Requirement | Behaviour | Test |
|---|---|---|
| REQ-ZONE-001 | build, init, learn, align (and --learn), place, advice, compare, boundaries, impact, usages, plain install, update --check, uninstall, the MCP server with --build-missing and every hook (with its background refresh) write only below &lt;project>/.wingbox/ and the user's home folder. Only init also updates the project's .gitignore. | `tests/test_write_zones.py::WriteZonesTest::test_every_command_writes_only_into_wingbox_and_the_home_folder` |
| REQ-ZONE-002 | portable.refuse_links and portable.open_unlinked raise LinkRefused (an OSError whose text names the link and says to remove it) when the path's `.wingbox` folder, a folder below it or, for open_unlinked, the file itself is a symbolic link, whether its target exists or not; nothing is created or changed at the target. A project that is reached through a linked folder above `.wingbox`, and a path in no `.wingbox`, are not judged. The check does not need O_NOFOLLOW (Windows); where O_NOFOLLOW exists, a file that becomes a link after the check is not followed either. | `tests/test_review_wp8_writes_install.py::LinkHelperTest::test_the_helper_refuses_a_link_at_wingbox_and_below_it_and_nothing_above` |
| REQ-ZONE-003 | with `.wingbox/REPORT.md` or `.wingbox/map.html` a symbolic link to a file outside the project (as a clone of a repository that commits such a link has it), `wingbox build` leaves the file outside byte for byte as it was, creates nothing outside, names the link in one error and exits with a status that is not 0; the graph itself is built.
